| Agreement | The following TP is endorsed for Rel-19 TS38.214, Clause 6.1.2.1 is endorsed.<br>The corresponding final CR for Rel-19 TS38.214, Clause 6.1.2.1 in R1-2608741 is endorsed.<br>- Reason for change: The OCC length parameter for Rel-19 NR NTN is not clearly captured in TS 38.214.<br>- Summary of change: For Rel-19 NR NTN, OCC length is provided by occ-LengthAndSequenceIndex for configured grant Type-1 PUSCH and occ-Length-r19 for DG PUSCH and configured grant Type 2 PUSCH.<br>- Consequences if not approved: Unclear UE behaviour<br>\| 6.1.2.1 Resource allocation in time domain *** Unchanged parts are omitted *** For a PUSCH transmission with repetition Type A, a UE considers OCC operation enabled if the PUSCH is it is configured with or indicated an OCC length, LOCC > 1. a configured grant Type 1 PUSCH with an OCC length LOCC provided by occ-LengthAndSequenceIndex, or a dynamic grant PUSCH or configured grant Type 2 PUSCH indicated by a DCI format indicating a TDRA row with an OCC length LOCC provided by occ-Length-r19. For a PUSCH transmission with repetition Type A and with OCC operation enabled, an OCC group is defined by Locc consecutive PUSCH repetitions and the integer number of OCC groups M is determined as M=K/Locc OCC group m includes PUSCH repetition m x Locc, m x Locc+1, .., (m+1) x Locc-1, where m=0,1, .., M-1, where repetition i within the OCC group will be multiplied with the OCC factor wi, where i is the repetition index within the OCC group. The OCC factor is for each repetition index i, based on the orthogonal sequence defined in Table 6.3.2.5A-1 and Table 6.3.2.5A-2 of [4, TS 38.211], where the sequence index n is derived from the ‘Antenna Port(s)’ field in the DCI format scheduling the transmission for PUSCH or activating configured grant Type 2 PUSCH according to [5, TS 38.212], or provided by OCCsequenceIndexCGType1-r19 for configured grant Type 1 PUSCH, and provided for actual transmission according to clause 6.3.1.2a in [4, TS 38.211], where i=0,1 for OCC length 2 and i=0,1,2,3 for OCC length 4. *** Unchanged parts are omitted *** \| | [Chair notes RAN1#126bis_eom1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23126bis_eom1.docx)<br>`[126bis-R19-AI 8.1]` |

| Agreement | Adopt the following TP to TS38.213 Section 11.1 in principle.<br>\| Reason for change: The SSB block is assumed to be confined within the DL subband. However, this assumption is not explicitly captured in the specification. If the SSB is configured in RBs outside the DL subband (e.g., in the guard band or UL subband), the UE behavior becomes ambiguous, and SSB reception performance may be negatively impacted. Summary of change: For SBFD symbols, it is specified that the SSB shall be confined within a DL subband. Consequences if not approved: UE behavior for SSB reception in SBFD symbols is unclear. \|<br>\| 11.1 Slot configuration < Unchanged Text Omitted > In an SBFD symbol, and except for cross-link interference measurements [6, TS 38.214], the UE transmits or receives only in RBs that are both in the active UL BWP and in the UL sub-band, or both in the active DL BWP and the DL sub-band(s), respectively. The UE does not transmit in SBFD symbols indicated for presence of SS/PBCH blocks within the active DL BWP by ssb-PositionsInBurst in SIB1 or in ServingCellConfigCommon. The UE assumes that the SS/PBCH blocks in SBFD symbols are within one DL subband. \|<br>Final CR in R1-2608789 is agreed. | [Chair notes RAN1#126bis_eom1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23126bis_eom1.docx)<br>[R1-2608621](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Docs/R1-2608621.zip)<br>`[126bis-R19-AI 8.2]` |
| Agreement | Adopt the following TP to TS38.214 Section 5.1.2.1 and 6.1.2.1 in principle.<br>\| Reason for change: In the legacy, for multi-PUSCH scheduling, the UE does not expect to be scheduled with one or multiple PUSCHs and each PUSCH collides with legacy DL symbols indicated by tdd-UL-DL-ConfigurationCommon or tdd-UL-DL-ConfigurationDedicated, or SSB symbols. However, if SBFD subbands are configured in legacy DL symbols, PUSCH transmission occasions in the DL symbols configured with SBFD subbands should be valid and allowed. In addition, a PUSCH transmission occasion colliding with symbol(s) that is not the valid symbol type or across two symbol types shall be invalid and the UE does not expect to receive such scheduling signalling where all scheduled PUSCHs are invalid. For multi-PDSCH scheduling, the UE does not expect to be scheduled with one or multiple PDSCHs and each PDSCH collides with legacy UL symbols indicated by tdd-UL-DL-ConfigurationCommon or tdd-UL-DL-ConfigurationDedicated. However, with introduction of SBFD, a PDSCH reception occasion colliding with symbol(s) that is not the valid symbol type or across two symbol types shall be invalid and the UE does not expect to receive such scheduling signalling where all scheduled PDSCHs are invalid. Summary of change: For multi-PUSCH scheduling, clarify that a scheduled PUSCH colliding with DL symbol(s) configured as SBFD symbol(s) is valid, and a scheduled PUSCH colliding with symbol(s) that is not the valid symbol type or across two symbol types is invalid. For multi-PDSCH scheduling, clarify that a scheduled PDSCH colliding with symbol(s) that is not the valid symbol type or across two symbol types is invalid. Consequences if not approved: Incorrect scheduling restriction for multi-PUSCH scheduling and multi-PDSCH scheduling in the specification. \|<br>\| *** Unchanged text omitted *** 5.1.2.1 Resource allocation in time domain *** Unchanged text omitted *** If a UE is configured with pdsch-TimeDomainAllocationListForMultiPDSCH or pdsch-TimeDomainAllocationListForMultiPDSCH-DCI-1-3 in which one or more rows contain multiple SLIVs for PDSCH on a DL BWP of a serving cell, the UE does not expect to be scheduled with one or multiple PDSCH receptions by a single DCI format 1_1 or DCI format 1_3, respectively, where each PDSCH reception overlaps with a UL symbol indicated by tdd-UL-DL-ConfigurationCommon or tdd-UL-DL-ConfigurationDedicated if provided, or is not in the valid symbol type if the UE is not configured with sbfd-Config2-Reception, or is mapped to both SBFD symbols and non-SBFD symbols within a slot. *** Unchanged text omitted *** 6.1.2.1 Resource allocation in time domain *** Unchanged text omitted *** If a UE is configured with pusch-TimeDomainAllocationListForMultiPUSCH or pusch-TimeDomainAllocationListForMultiPUSCH-DCI-0-3 in which one or more rows contain multiple SLIVs for PUSCH on a UL BWP of a serving cell, the UE does not expect to be scheduled with one or multiple PUSCH transmissions by a single DCI format 0_1 or 0_3, where each PUSCH transmission overlaps with a DL symbol indicated by tdd-UL-DL-ConfigurationCommon or tdd-UL-DL-ConfigurationDedicated if provided, and not indicated as SBFD by tdd-UL-DL-ConfigurationCommon if applicable, or a symbol of an SS/PBCH block with index provided by ssb-PositionsInBurst, or is not in the valid symbol type if the UE is not configured with sbfd-Config2-Transmission, or is mapped to both SBFD symbols and non-SBFD symbols within a slot. *** Unchanged text omitted *** \|<br>Final CR in R1-2608758 is agreed. | [Chair notes RAN1#126bis_eom1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23126bis_eom1.docx)<br>[R1-2608621](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Docs/R1-2608621.zip)<br>`[126bis-R19-AI | Agreement | Adopt the following TP to TS38.214 as alignment CR<br>\| ---------------------------------------------------------- Start of text proposal --------------------------------------------------- 5.1 UE procedure for receiving the physical downlink shared channel <unchanged text omitted> If more than one PDSCH on a serving cell each without a corresponding PDCCH transmission are in a slot, after resolving overlapping with symbols in the slot indicated as uplink by tdd-UL-DL-ConfigurationCommon, or by tdd-UL-DL-ConfigurationDedicated or across both SBFD symbols and non-SBFD symbols as described in 5.1.2.1a, or in invalid symbol type if the UE is not configured with sbfd-Config2-Reception as described in 5.1.2.1a, or determined as non-active periods of cell DTX, if the serving cell is activated with cell DTX, based on [10, TS 38.321], or not available for PDSCH without a corresponding PDCCH transmission receptions as described in clause 24 of [6, TS 38.213], a UE receives one or more PDSCHs without corresponding PDCCH transmissions in the slot as specified below. ‒ Step 0: set j=0, where j is the number of selected PDSCH(s) for decoding. Q is the set of activated PDSCHs without corresponding PDCCH transmissions within the slot ‒ Step 1: A UE receives one PDSCH with the lowest configured sps-ConfigIndex within Q, set j=j+1. Designate the received PDSCH as survivor PDSCH. ‒ Step 2: The survivor PDSCH in step 1 and any other PDSCH(s) overlapping (even partially) with the survivor PDSCH in step 1 are excluded from Q. ‒ Step 3: Repeat step 1 and 2 until Q is empty or j is equal to the number of unicast/multicast PDSCHs in a slot supported by the UE. A UE capable of PDSCH repetitions for broadcast channels, which assumed the DCI format 1_0 in the Type0 PDCCH CSS of searchSpaceZero transmitted with two inter-slot repetitions may assume that PDSCHs scheduled by the DCI format 1_0 have also been transmitted with inter-slot repetitions in the same slots as the Type0 PDCCH CSS, with the same RV as indicated by the DCI format 1_0. For a cell detected in cell search procedure with synchronization raster defined in Table 5.4.3.1-2 or Table 5.4.3.1-3 of [8, TS 38.101-1] or Table 5.4.3.1-2 of [22, TS 38.101-5], the size of CORESET 0 for the cell in this clause refers to the size of punctured CORESET 0 as defined in clause 7.3.2.2 of [4, TS 38.211] if any. <unchanged text omitted> 5.1.2.2 Resource allocation in frequency domain <unchanged text omitted> If a UE is configured with sbfd-Config2-Reception, only the assigned PRBs that are both in the active DL BWP and in the DL sub-band(s) are used for a PDSCH reception in SBFD symbols scheduled without corresponding PDCCH transmission using sps-Config and activated by DCI format 1_0, 1_1 or 1_2, or PDSCH receptions in SBFD symbols across different slots scheduled by a DCI using pdsch-TimeDomainAllocationListForMultiPDSCH in which one or more rows contain multiple SLIVs or using pdsch-AggregationFactor or using repetitionNumber. <unchanged text omitted> 6.1.2.2.1 Uplink resource allocation type 0 <unchanged text omitted> If a UE is configured with SBFD symbols, - only the resource blocks that are both in the active UL BWP and in the UL sub-band are used for PUSCH transmission in SBFD symbol(s). For Type 2 PUSCH transmissions with a configured grant and PUSCH transmissions scheduled by DCI format 0_1, 0_2 or 0_3, is provided by sbfd-Config2-PUSCH-RBOffset per UL BWP in . If not provided, . - The UE does not expect that the PRBs for PUSCH transmissions in SBFD symbols with to be overlapped with PRBs outside the PRBs that are both in the active UL BWP and in the UL sub-band. 6.1.2.2.2 Uplink resource allocation type 1 In uplink resource allocation of type 1, the resource block assignment information indicates to a scheduled UE a set of contiguously allocated non-interleaved virtual resource blocks within the active bandwidth part of size PRBs except for the case when DCI format 0_0 is decoded in any common search space in which case the size of the initial UL bandwidth part shall be used. | Agreement | Adopt the following TP for TS38.214 in principle<br>\| Reason for change: nCSI_ref is determined based on the number of CSI-RS/SSB resources. For L1-CLI-RSSI report, the CLI is measured based on the CLI-RSSI resource rather than CSI-RS/SSB resources. The existing rule for nCSI_ref determination is not applicable for L1 CLI-RSSI report. Summary of change: Clarify nCSI_ref for L1-CLI-RSSI report as follows: If a single CLI-RSSI measurement resource is configured for interference measurement, nCSI_ref is the smallest value greater than or equal to , such that it corresponds to a valid downlink slot. If multiple CLI-RSSI measurement resources are configured for interference measurement, nCSI_ref is the smallest value greater than or equal to , such that it corresponds to a valid downlink slot. Consequences if not approved: UE behavior for nCSI_ref determination of LI-CLI-RSSI report is unclear. \|<br>\| 5.2.2.5 CSI reference resource definition The CSI reference resource for a serving cell is defined as follows: - In the frequency domain, the CSI reference resource is defined by the group of downlink physical resource blocks corresponding to the band to which the derived CSI relates. - In the time domain, the CSI reference resource for a CSI reporting in uplink slot n', or a CSI reporting in an OCC group starting in uplink slot n' if OCC is enabled, is defined by a single downlink slot , where is a parameter configured by higher layer as specified in clause 4.2 of [6, TS 38.213], and where is the subcarrier spacing configuration for with a value of 0 for frequency range 1 and for FR2-NTN, - where and and are the subcarrier spacing configurations for DL and UL, respectively, and and are determined by higher-layer configured ca-SlotOffset for the cells transmitting the uplink and downlink, as defined in clause 4.5 of [4, TS 38.211] - where for periodic and semi-persistent CSI reporting or for periodic CLI RSSI reporting - if a single CSI-RS/SSB resource is configured for channel measurement or if a single CLI-RSSI measurement resource is configured for interference measurement, nCSI_ref is the smallest value greater than or equal to , such that it corresponds to a valid downlink slot, or - if multiple CSI-RS/SSB resources are configured for channel measurement or if multiple CLI-RSSI measurement resources are configured for interference measurement, nCSI_ref is the smallest value greater than or equal to , such that it corresponds to a valid downlink slot. If a CSI-ReportConfig is configured with the higher layer parameter codebookType set to 'typeI-SinglePanel-r19', with total number of CSI-RS ports , for capability 2, as reported by UE capability indication, nCSI_ref is the smallest value greater than or equal to , such that it corresponds to a valid downlink slot. \|<br>Final CR in R1-2608747 is agreed. | [Chair notes RAN1#126bis_eom1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23126bis_eom1.docx)<br>[R1-2608698](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Docs/R1-2608698.zip)<br>`[126bis-R19-AI 8.2]` |
| Agreement | Adopt the following TP to TS38.214 as alignment CR<br>\| ==============First change============== ==============End of change============== \| | [Chair notes RAN1#126bis_eom1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23126bis_eom1.docx)<br>[R1-2608740](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Docs/R1-2608740.zip)<br>`[126bis-R19-AI 8.2]` |
| Agreement | Draft CR in R1-2608748 is agreed in principle.<br>Final CR in R1-2608756 is agreed.<br>LP-WUS | [Chair notes RAN1#126bis_eom1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23126bis_eom1.docx)<br>[R1-2608748](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Docs/R1-2608748.zip)<br>`[126bis-R19-AI 8.2]` |
| Agreement | Adopt the following TP to TS 38.211 clause 7.2 in principle<br>\| Reason for change: \| The antenna port number for LP-SS and LP-WUS is not defined, but it is cited in the spec TS38.211. \|<br>\|  \|  \|<br>\| Summary of change: \| Add the description in TS38.211 to clarify that the antenna port number for LP-WUS and LP-SS is 6000 and 7000 separately. \|<br>\|  \|  \|<br>\| Consequences if not approved: \| The antenna port for LP-SS and LP-WUS is not clear. \|<br>\| *** Unchanged parts are omitted *** 7.2 Physical resources The frame structure and physical resources the UE shall assume when receiving downlink transmissions are defined in Clause 4. The following antenna ports are defined for the downlink: - Antenna ports starting with 1000 for PDSCH - Antenna ports starting with 2000 for PDCCH - Antenna ports starting with 3000 for channel-state information reference signals - Antenna ports starting with 4000 for SS/PBCH block transmission - Antenna ports starting with 5000 for positioning reference signals The UE shall not assume that two antenna ports are quasi co-located with respect to any QCL type unless specified otherwise. For DM-RS associated with a PDSCH, the channel over which a PDSCH symbol on one antenna port is conveyed can be inferred from the channel over which a DM-RS symbol on the same antenna port is conveyed only if the two symbols are within the same resource as the scheduled PDSCH, in the same slot, and in the same PRG as described in clause 5.1.2.3 of [6, TS 38.214]. For DM-RS associated with a PDCCH, the channel over which a PDCCH symbol on one antenna port is conveyed can be inferred from the channel over which a DM-RS symbol on the same antenna port is conveyed only if the two symbols are within resources for which the UE may assume the same precoding being used as described in clause 7.3.2.2. For DM-RS associated with a PBCH, the channel over which a PBCH symbol on one antenna port is conveyed can be inferred from the channel over which a DM-RS symbol on the same antenna port is conveyed only if the two symbols are within a SS/PBCH block transmitted within the same slot, and with the same block index according to clause 7.4.3.1. *** Unchanged parts are omitted *** \|<br>Final CR in R1-2608749 is agreed. | [Chair notes RAN1#126bis_eom1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23126bis_eom1.docx)<br>[R1-2608700](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Docs/R1-2608700.zip)<br>`[126bis-R19-AI 8.2]` |
| Agreement | Adopt the following TP to TS 38.214 clause 5.1.5 in principle:<br>\| Reason for change: \| RAN1 agreements supported Type C (and Type D if applicable) as the QCL type for WUS in RRC_CONNECTED mode, which is applicable when unified TCI framework is configured. TS 38.213 correctly reflected the agreements, while TS 38.214 is not consistent with TS 38.213 and the agreements. Also, several occasions with unified TCI framework also need to be clarified to be applicable to WUS. \|<br>\|  \|  \|<br>\| Summary of change: \| When applying the unified TCI framework, separate description for WUS to use Type C QCL type is added. \|<br>\|  \|  \|<br>\| Consequences if not approved: \| The UE behaviour in TS 38.214 is not consistent with TS 38.213 or the RAN1 agreements. \|<br>\| =============================== Start of TP================================ 5.1.5 Antenna ports quasi co-location The UE can be configured with a list of up to M TCI-State configurations within the higher layer parameter PDSCH-Config to decode PDSCH according to a detected PDCCH with DCI intended for the UE and the given serving cell, where M depends on the UE capability maxNumberConfiguredTCIstatesPerCC. Each TCI-State contains parameters for configuring a quasi co-location relationship between one or two downlink reference signals and the DM-RS ports of the PDSCH, the DM-RS port of PDCCH or the CSI-RS port(s) of a CSI-RS resource. The quasi co-location relationship is configured by the higher layer parameter qcl-Type1 for the first DL RS, and qcl-Type2 for the second DL RS (if configured). For the case of two DL RSs, the QCL types shall not be the same, regardless of whether the references are to the same DL RS or different DL RSs. The quasi co-location types corresponding to each DL RS are given by the higher layer parameter qcl-Type in QCL-Info and may take one of the following values: - 'typeA': {Doppler shift, Doppler spread, average delay, delay spread} - 'typeB': {Doppler shift, Doppler spread} - 'typeC': {Doppler shift, average delay} - 'typeD': {Spatial Rx parameter} The UE can be configured with a list of up to 128 TCI-State configurations, within the higher layer parameter dl-OrJointTCI-StateList in PDSCH-Config for providing a reference signal for the quasi co-location for DM-RS of PDSCH and DM-RS of PDCCH in a BWP/CC, for CSI-RS, and to provide a reference signal with qcl-Type set to 'typeD', if applicable, for determining UL TX spatial filter for dynamic-grant and configured-grant based PUSCH and PUCCH resource in a BWP/CC, and SRS. If the TCI-State or TCI-UL-State configurations are absent in a BWP of the CC, the UE can apply the TCI-State or TCI-UL-State configurations from a reference BWP of a reference CC configured by unifiedTCI-StateRef. The UE is not expected to be configured with tci-StatesToAddModList, SpatialRelationInfo or PUCCH-SpatialRelationInfo, except SpatialRelationInfoPos in a CC in a band, if the UE is configured with dl-OrJointTCI-StateList or ul-TCI-StateList in any CC in the same band. The UE can assume that when the UE is configured with tci-StatesToAddModList in any CC in the CC list configured by simultaneousTCI-UpdateList1-r16, simultaneousTCI-UpdateList2-r16, simultaneousSpatial-UpdatedList1-r16, or simultaneousSpatial-UpdatedList2-r16, the UE is not configured with dl-OrJointTCI-StateList or ul-TCI-StateList in any CC within the same band in the CC list. The UE receives an activation command, as described in clause 6.1.3.14 of [10, TS 38.321], or 6.1.3.47 of [10, TS 38.321], used to map up to 8 TCI states and/or pairs of TCI states, with one TCI state for DL channels/signals and/or one TCI state for UL channels/signals to the codepoints of the DCI field 'Transmission Configuration Indication' for one or for a set of CCs/DL BWPs. When a set of TCI state IDs are activated for a set of CCs/DL BWPs and if applicable, for a set of CCs/UL BWPs, where the applicable list of CCs is determined by the indicated CC in the activation command, the same set of TCI state IDs are applied for all DL and/or UL BWPs in the indicated CCs. If the activation command maps TCI-State(s) and/or TCI-UL-State(s) to only one TCI codepoint, the UE shall apply the indicated TCI-State(s) and/or TCI-UL-State(s) to one or to a set of CCs /DL BWPs, and if applicable, to one or to a set of CCs /UL BWPs once the indicated mapping for the one single TCI codepoint is applied as described in [11, TS 38.133]. When the bwp-id or cell for QCL-TypeA/D source RS in a QCL-Info of the TCI state is not configured, the UE assumes that QCL-TypeA/D source RS is configured in the CC/DL BWP where TCI state applies. When tci-PresentInDCI is set as 'enabled' or tci-PresentDCI-1-2 is configured for the CORESET, a UE configured with dl-OrJointTCI-StateList with activated TCI-State or ul-TCI-StateList with activated TCI-UL-State receives DCI format 1_1/1_2/1_3 providing indicated TCI-State(s) and/or TCI-UL-State(s) for a CC or all CCs in the same CC list configured by simultaneousU-TCI-UpdateList1-r17, simultaneousU-TCI-UpdateList2-r17, simultaneousU-TCI-UpdateList3-r17, simultaneousU-TCI-UpdateList4-r17. The DCI format 1_3 provides indicated TCI state(s) and/or TCI-UL-State(s) for the CC(s) in a scheduledCellListDCI-1-3 if the UE is scheduled by the DCI format 1_3 to receive PDSCH at least on one serving cell in the scheduledCellListDCI-1-3. The DCI format 1_1/1_2 can be with or without, if applicable, DL assignment. If the DCI format 1_1/1_2 is without DL assignment, the UE can assume the following: - CS-RNTI is used to scramble the CRC for the DCI - The values of the following DCI fields are set as follows: - RV = all '1's - MCS = all '1's - NDI = 0 - Set to all '0's for FDRA Type 0, or all '1's for FDRA Type 1, or all '0's for dynamicSwitch (same as in Table 10.2-4 of [6, TS 38.213]). After a UE receives an initial higher layer configuration of dl-OrJointTCI-StateList where more than one TCI-State can be used as an indicated TCI state and before application of an indicated TCI state from the configured TCI states: - The UE assumes that DM-RS of PDSCH and DM-RS of PDCCH that are not received during the RACH procedure, and the CSI-RS applying the indicated TCI state are quasi co-located with the reference signal(s) in the CandidateTCI-State indicated in the LTM Cell Switch Command MAC CE [10, 38.321] if applicable. - The UE assumes that DM-RS of PDSCH and DM-RS of PDCCH and the CSI-RS applying the indicated TCI state are quasi co-located with the reference signal in the determined CandidateTCI-State for RACH-less conditional LTM cell switch [10, 38.321] if applicable. - The UE assumes that DM-RS of PDSCH and DM-RS of PDCCH and the CSI-RS applying the indicated TCI state are quasi co-located with the SS/PBCH block the UE identified during the random access procedure initiated by RACH-based conditional LTM cell switch if applicable. - Otherwise, the UE assumes that DM-RS of PDSCH and DM-RS of PDCCH, and the CSI-RS applying the indicated TCI state are quasi co-located with the SS/PBCH block the UE identified during the initial access procedure with respect to qcl-Type set to 'typeA', and when applicable, also with respect to qcl-Type set to 'typeD'. . After a UE receives an initial higher layer configuration of dl-OrJointTCI-StateList where more than one TCI-State can be used as an indicated TCI state or an initial higher layer configuration of ul-TCI-StateList where more than one TCI-UL-State can be used as an indicated TCI state and before application of an indicated TCI state from the configured TCI states: - The UE determines the UL TX spatial filter, if applicable, for dynamic-grant based PUSCH that is not transmitted during the RACH procedure and configured-grant based PUSCH and PUCCH that are not transmitted during the RACH procedure, and for SRS applying the indicated TCI state, from the CandidateTCI-State or CandidateTCI-UL-State indicated in the LTM Cell Switch Command MAC CE [10, 38.321] if applicable. - The UE determines the UL TX spatial filter, if applicable, for dynamic-grant and configured-grant based PUSCH and PUCCH, and for SRS applying the indicated TCI state, from the determined CandidateTCI-State or CandidateTCI-UL-State for RACH-less conditional LTM cell switch [10, 38.321] if applicable. - The UE determines the UL TX spatial filter, if applicable, for dynamic-grant and configured-grant based PUSCH and PUCCH, and for SRS applying the indicated TCI state, is the same as that for a PUSCH transmission scheduled by a RAR UL grant or a MsgA PUSCH transmission during the random access procedure initiated by RACH-based conditional LTM cell switch [10, 38.321] if applicable. - Otherwise, the UE assumes that the UL TX spatial filter, if applicable, for dynamic-grant and configured-grant based PUSCH and PUCCH, and for SRS applying the indicated TCI state, is the same as that for a PUSCH transmission scheduled by a RAR UL grant or a MsgA PUSCH transmission during the initial access procedure. After a UE receives a higher layer configuration of dl-OrJointTCI-StateList where more than one TCI-State can be used as an indicated TCI state as part of a Reconfiguration with sync procedure as described in [12, TS 38.331] and before applying an indicated TCI state from the configured TCI states: - The UE assumes that DM-RS of PDSCH and DM-RS of PDCCH, and the CSI-RS applying the indicated TCI state are quasi co-located with the SS/PBCH block or the CSI-RS resource the UE identified during the random access procedure initiated by the Reconfiguration with sync procedure as described in [12, TS 38.331] with respect to qcl-Type set to 'typeA', and when applicable, also with respect to qcl-Type set to 'typeD'. After a UE receives a higher layer configuration of dl-OrJointTCI-StateList where more than one TCI-State can be used as an indicated TCI state or a higher layer configuration of ul-TCI-StateList where more than one TCI-UL-State can be used as an indicated TCI state as part of a Reconfiguration with sync procedure as described in [12, TS 38.331] and before applying an indicated TCI state from the configured TCI states: - The UE assumes that the UL TX spatial filter, if applicable, for dynamic-grant and configured-grant based PUSCH and PUCCH, and for SRS applying the indicated TCI state, is the same as that for a PUSCH transmission scheduled by a RAR UL grant or a MsgA PUSCH transmission during random access procedure initiated by the Reconfiguration with sync procedure as described in [12, TS 38.331]. If a UE receives a higher layer configuration of dl-OrJointTCI-StateList where only one TCI-State can be used as an indicated TCI state, the UE obtains the QCL assumptions from that TCI state for DM-RS of PDSCH and DM-RS of PDCCH, and the CSI -RS applying the indicated TCI state. If a UE receives a higher layer configuration of dl-OrJointTCI-StateList where only one TCI-State can be used as an indicated TCI state or a higher layer configuration of ul-TCI-StateList where only one TCI-UL-State can be used as an indicated TCI state, the UE determines an UL TX spatial filter, if applicable, from that TCI state for dynamic-grant and configured-grant based PUSCH and PUCCH, and SRS applying the indicated TCI state. ============================= Unchanged Text Omitted ============================ ============================= End of TP \|<br>Final CR in R1-2608750 is agreed.<br>LTM | [Chair notes RAN1#126bis_eom1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23126bis_eom1.docx)<br>[R1-2608726](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Docs/R1-2608726.zip)<br>`[126bis-R19-AI 8.2]` |
| Agreement | Adopt the following TP to TS38.214 Section 5.2.4a in principle<br>\| Reason for change: \| Clause 5.2.4a specifies early CSI acquisition for both LTM and L3 handover. In the first paragraph, "candidate cell" is used for LTM and "target cell" is used for handover. However, in the remaining part of the clause, the CSI-IM association, the number of NZP-CSI-RS resources for channel measurement, and the PUSCH used for the CSI report are described only for a candidate cell, although the same procedures apply to the target cell for handover. \|<br>\|  \|  \|<br>\| Summary of change: \| In clause 5.2.4a, "or the target cell" is added where only "candidate cell" is used, so that the early CSI reporting procedure applies to both LTM and handover. \|<br>\|  \|  \|<br>\| Consequences if not approved: \| The early CSI reporting procedure for handover is not fully specified in clause 5.2.4a, since the CSI-IM association and the PUSCH for the CSI report are described only for a candidate cell. This may lead to ambiguous or incorrect implementation of early CSI reporting for handover. \|<br>\| ==============First change============== 5.2.4a CSI Reporting for LTM and handover A UE configured with LTM-Config can be provided configurations for CSI acquisition, by up to one Reporting Setting, ltm-CSI-ReportConfig, for a candidate cell. A UE can be provided configuration for CSI acquisition, by one Reporting Setting, earlyCSI-Acquisition in ReconfigurationWithSync, for a target cell. Each Reporting Setting ltm-CSI-ReportConfig or earlyCSI-Acquisition is associated with either one or two Resource Settings - When one Resource Setting (given by higher layer parameter ltm-ResourcesForChannelMeasurement or early-NZP-CSI-RS-ResourceSet) is configured, it provides a list of NZP CSI-RS resources for both channel and interference measurements. The UE is not expected to be configured with more than 128 NZP CSI-RS ports in the CSI-RS resource set contained within the Resource Setting. - When two Resource Settings are configured, the first Resource Setting (given by higher layer parameter ltm-ResourcesForChannelMeasurement or early-NZP-CSI-RS-ResourceSet) provides a list of NZP CSI-RS resources for channel measurement, and the second Resource Setting (given by higher layer parameter ltm-ResourceForInterferenceMeasurements or early-CSI-IM-ResourceSet), provides a list of CSI-IM resources for interference measurement. The UE is not expected to be configured with more than 128 NZP CSI-RS ports in the CSI-RS resource set contained within the Resource Setting. The Resource Setting given by higher layer parameter ltm-ResourcesForChannelMeasurement, LTM-CSI-ResourceConfig, contains configuration of a LTM-NZP-CSI-RS-ResourceSet which comprises of a list of Z ≥ 1 NZP CSI-RS resource indices (given by ltm-CSI-RS-ResourceList) and a list of Z LTM-CandidateIds (given by ltm-CandidateIdList) referring to candidate cells associated with the NZP CSI-RS resource indices. For CSI acquisition associated with a Reporting Setting, ltm-CSI-ReportConfig, the UE is expected to measure the NZP-CSI-RS resources in ltm-CSI-RS-ResourceList associated with the LTM-CandidateId that is equal to the LTM-CandidateId of the LTM-Candidate under which the Reporting Setting is configured. If interference measurement is performed on CSI-IM, each NZP-CSI-RS resource the UE is expected to measure for channel measurement for a candidate cell is resource-wise associated with a CSI-IM resource for the same candidate cell by the ordering of the NZP-CSI-RS resources and CSI-IM resources in the corresponding resource sets. The number of NZP-CSI-RS resources the UE is expected to measure for channel measurement for a candidate cell equals to the number of CSI-IM resources for interference measurement. The UE shall expect the following configuration provided by ltm-CSI-ReportConfig or earlyCSI-Acquisition: - For the frequency granularity of the CSI report, the CQI format indicator is Wideband CQI. - For the frequency granularity of the CSI report, the PMI format indicator is Wideband PMI. - The codebook type is typeI-SinglePanel. - The reportQuantity is set to ‘cri-RI-PMI-CQI’. After a UE receives an LTM Cell Switch Command MAC CE [10, TS 38.321] providing a candidate cell (given by Target Configuration ID field), and a ltm-CSI-ReportConfig is configured for the candidate cell, the UE can measure corresponding NZP CSI-RS resources and CSI-IM resources if configured/activated, and shall transmit a CSI report to the candidate cell. After a UE receives a ReconfigurationWithSync configured with earlyCSI-Acquisition, the UE can measure corresponding NZP CSI-RS resources and CSI-IM resources if configured/activated, and shall transmit a CSI report to the target cell. For RACH-less LTM cell switch or RACH-less handover [23, TS 38.300], the UE shall transmit the CSI report to the candidate cell using the first PUSCH corresponding to a dynamic grant or a configured grant [6, TS 38.213]. For RACH-based LTM cell switch or RACH-based handover using a contention-free random access procedure [23, TS 38.300], the UE shall transmit the CSI report to the candidate cell using the PUSCH scheduled by the RAR UL grant or MsgA PUSCH. For RACH-based LTM cell switch or RACH-based handover using a contention-based random access procedure [23, TS 38.300], the UE shall transmit the CSI report to the candidate cell using the first PUSCH corresponding to a dynamic grant or a configured grant after the HARQ-ACK transmission having ACK value corresponding to PDSCH scheduled by a PDCCH transmission addressed to the C-RNTI that is received in response to MSG A, or the first PUSCH scheduled by a PDCCH transmission addressed to the C-RNTI, received in response to Msg3 or MsgA. For the PUSCH determined for CSI reporting, when the UE applies PUSCH repetition Type A or Type B, the CSI report is transmitted only on the first repetition for PUSCH repetition Type A, or on the first actual repetition for PUSCH repetition Type B. If a valid CSI is not available, the UE shall transmit a CSI report which contains a CQI corresponding to the lowest CQI index. Depending on the UE capability, the UE may measure NZP CSI-RS resources and CSI-IM resources if configured/activated corresponding to a ltm-CSI-ReportConfig before receiving the LTM Cell Switch Command MAC CE [10, TS 38.321]. ==============End of change============== \|<br>Final CR in R1-2608766 is agreed. | [Chair notes RAN1#126bis_eom1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23126bis_eom1.docx)<br>[R1-2608653](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Docs/R1-2608653.zip)<br>`[126bis-R19-AI 8.2]` |
| Agreement | Adopt the following TP to TS38.214 Section 5.2.1.4.2 in principle<br>\| Reason for change: \| SSBRI and CRI (the highlighted part below) are used in event triggered L1 measurement report (TS 38.321 clause 5.35.1, 5.35.4, 6.1.3.84), which is associated with LTM-CSI-ReportConfig with ltm-ReportConfigType set to ‘eventTriggered’ and with reportquantity set to ssb-index-RSRP or cri-RSRP (the highlighted part below). However SSBRI and CRI (with definition highlighted below) are not defined when ltm-ReportConfigType is set to ‘eventTriggered’ (the highlighted condition below), as from RAN1 TS 38.214 clause 5.2.1.4.2: A CR to TS 38.321 was submitted to RAN2-135 meeting, it was discussed in RAN2, and the conclusion is ‘Noted. Interesting company can submit RAN1 correction’. \|<br>\|  \|  \|<br>\| Summary of change: \| Add definition of SSBRI and CRI for the case that ltm-ReportConfigType set to ‘eventTriggered’. \|<br>\|  \|  \|<br>\| Consequences if not approved: \| It remains unclear which index of SSB / CSI-RS is referred by SSBRI / CRI. \|<br>\| ==============First change============== 5.2.1.4.2 Report quantity configurations <Unchanged part omitted> If a UE is configured with a ltm-CSI-ReportConfig with ltm-ReportConfigType which is set to ‘periodic’ or ‘semiPersistentOnPUCCH’ or ‘semiPersistentOnPUSCH’ or ‘aperiodic’, - if the UE is configured with spCellInclusion with reportQuantity set to 'ssb-Index-RSRP', the UE shall report in a single reporting instance nrOfReportedRS-PerCell different SSBRI for the current SpCell and each of the nrOfReportedCells -1 candidate cells. Otherwise, the UE shall report in a single reporting instance nrOfReportedRS-PerCell different SSBRI for each of the nrOfReportedCells candidate cells, - where SSBRI k (k ≥ 0) corresponds to the configured (k+1)-th entry of the associated ltm-SSB-ResourceList in the corresponding ltm-SSB-ResourceSet, - if spCellInclusion is configured, SSB resources in ltm-SSB-ResourceList associated with the current SpCell are the entries where PCI (given by ltm-CandidatePCI) and frequency information (given by ssb-Frequency) of the candidate cell associated with the LTM-CandidateId (given by the corresponding entry in ltm-CandidateIdList) is equal to the PCI and center frequency of cell-defining SSB of the current SpCell. - if the UE is configured with spCellInclusion with reportQuantity set to 'cri-RSRP’, the UE shall report in a single reporting instance nrOfReportedRS-PerCell different CRI for the current SpCell and each of the nrOfReportedCells -1 candidate cells. Otherwise, the UE shall report in a single reporting instance nrOfReportedRS-PerCell different CRI for each of the nrOfReportedCells candidate cells, - where CRI k (k ≥ 0) corresponds to the configured (k+1)-th entry of the associated ltm-CSI-RS-ResourceList in the corresponding LTM-NZP-CSI-RS-ResourceSet, - if spCellInclusion is configured, NZP-CSI-RS resources in ltm-CSI-RS-ResourceList associated with the current SpCell are the entries where PCI (given by ltm-CandidatePCI) and frequency information (given by ssb-Frequency for the SSBs QCLed with NZP-CSI-RSs) of the candidate cell associated with the LTM-CandidateId (given by the corresponding entry in ltm-CandidateIdList)) is equal to the PCI and center frequency of cell-defining SSB of the current SpCell. If the UE is configured with a CSI-ReportConfig that contains a list of sub-configurations provided by csi-ReportSubConfigToAddModList, the UE can only be configured with NZP CSI-RS for interference measurement if each sub-configuration is configured with powerOffset and not configured with portSubsetIndicator. ==============End of change============== \|<br>Final CR in R1-2608767 is agreed.<br>XR Phase 3 | [Chair notes RAN1#126bis_eom1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23126bis_eom1.docx)<br>[R1-2608653](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Docs/R1-2608653.zip)<br>`[126bis-R19-AI 8.2]` |
| Agreement | Adopt the following TP to TS38.214 as alignment CR<br>\| ==============First change============== 10.6 Indication for cancelation of RRM measurement gaps/restrictions A measurement gap cancellation field ain a DCI format [5, TS 38.212] provided by a PDCCH reception is associated to an earliest RRM measurement gap occasion that starts at least 3 msec or 5 msec, as determined by a reported UE capability, after the end of the PDCCH reception. The indication by the measurement gap cancellation field is applied to the associated RRM measurement gap occasion on all cells in the applicable range of the measurement gap occasion that include the one or more scheduled cells associated with the DCI format [10, TS 38.133]. A UE can be indicated by a value ‘1’ of a measurement gap cancellation field in a DCI format provided by a PDCCH reception to cancel an associated RRM measurement gap occasion. If a UE indicates “Option 1” for , the UE ignores a value ‘0’ of the measurement gap cancellation field in a DCI format. If a UE indicates “Option 2” for , a value ‘0’ of the measurement gap cancellation field in a DCI format provided by a PDCCH reception indicates to the UE that the UE behavior for the associated RRM measurement gap occasion is as described in [10, TS 38.133]. When the UE detects more than one DCI formats that include the measurement gap cancellation field and are associated with a same associated RRM measurement gap occasion, the UE applies the indication provided by the measurement gap cancellation field of a last DCI format, as described in Clause 9, from the more than one DCI formats. ==============End of change============== \|<br>ISAC Channel model | [Chair notes RAN1#126bis_eom1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23126bis_eom1.docx)<br>[R1-2608014](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Docs/R1-2608014.zip)<br>`[126bis-R19-AI 8.2]` |

| Agreement | For the transmission parameter of TBS for the remaining part of broadcast information, which is indicated in the first part, RAN1 select from the followings<br>- Option 1: 7 bits indication (Same as Rel-19)<br>- Option 2: less than 7 bits indication with reduced TBS value set from Rel-19<br>  - FFS: details of the reduced TBS value set and indication | [Chair notes RAN1#126bis_eom1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23126bis_eom1.docx) |
| Agreement | For the transmission parameters of M value, FEC and repetition for the remaining part of broadcast information, which are indicated in the first part, RAN1 select from the followings<br>- Option 2: 4-bits solution<br>  - M value is indicated separately<br>    - 2 bits indication of M values from {2, 6, 12, 24}<br>  - FEC and repetition are jointly indicated<br>    - 2 bits joint indication from restricted combination of FEC and repetition as<br>\| 2 bits codepoint \| FEC \| Repetition \|<br>\| Codepoint 1 \| Disabled \| 1 \|<br>\| Codepoint 2 \| Code rate 1/3 \| 1 \|<br>\| Codepoint 3 \| Code rate 1/3 \| 2 \|<br>\| Codepoint 4 \| Code rate 1/3 \| 4 \|<br>- Option 3: 3-bits solution<br>  - M value, FEC and repetition are jointly indicated<br>    - 3 bits joint indication from restricted combination of M value, FEC and repetition<br>      - FFS: details<br>\| 3 bits codepoint \| M value \| FEC \| Repetition \|<br>\| Codepoint 1 \| 12 \| Disabled \| 1 \|<br>\| Codepoint 2 \| 6 \| Disabled \| 1 \|<br>\| Codepoint 3 \| 12 \| Code rate 1/3 \| 1 \|<br>\| Codepoint 4 \| 2 \| Disabled \| 1 \|<br>\| Codepoint 5 \| 6 \| Code rate 1/3 \| 2 \|<br>\| Codepoint 6 \| 2 \| Code rate 1/3 \| 1 \|<br>\| Codepoint 7 \| 2 \| Code rate 1/3 \| 2 \|<br>\| Codepoint 8 \| 2 \| Code rate 1/3 \| 4 \|<br>- Option 4: 2-bits solution<br>  - FEC is pre-defined as code rate 1/3<br>  - M value and repetition are jointly indicated<br>    - 2 bits joint indication from restricted combination of M value and repetition<br>      - FFS: details<br>\| 2 bits codepoint \| M value \| Repetition \|<br>\| Codepoint 1 \| 6 \| 1 \|<br>\| Codepoint 2 \| 2 \| 1 \|<br>\| Codepoint 3 \| 2 \| 2 \|<br>\| Codepoint 4 \| 2 \| 4 \|<br>- Proponent companies are encouraged to provide details for Option 3 or 4 within RAN1#126bis | [Chair notes RAN1#126bis_eom1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23126bis_eom1.docx) |
| Agreement | For the transmission periodicity of the remaining part of broadcast information, which is indicated in the first part, RAN1 select from the followings<br>- Option 1: 1 bit indication from {4, 16}<br>- Option 2: 2 bits indication from {4, 8, 12, 16} | [Chair notes RAN1#126bis_eom1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23126bis_eom1.docx) |
| Agreement | At least the followings are indicated in the remaining part of the broadcast information,<br>- Transmission parameters of the sequence-based SIP<br>- Transmission parameters of the L1 R2D control information | [Chair notes RAN1#126bis_eom1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23126bis_eom1.docx) |
| Agreement | For R2D, for the transmission of periodic unmodulated sinusoid single tone CFO calibration signal<br>- The periodicity is<br>  - Option P1: same as the pre-defined transmission periodicity (Psync) of R2D periodic sync signal | [Chair notes RAN1#126bis_eom1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23126bis_eom1.docx)<br>[R1-2607512](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Docs/R1-2607512.zip) |
| Agreement | For R2D, for the transmission of periodic unmodulated sinusoid single tone CFO calibration signal<br>  - The time location(s) is immediately after a first part of periodic broadcast information transmission including padding if any (when the first part is not followed by a remaining part), or immediately after a remaining part of periodic broadcast information transmission including padding if any (when a first part is followed by the remaining part) | [Chair notes RAN1#126bis_eom1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23126bis_eom1.docx)<br>[R1-2607512](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Docs/R1-2607512.zip) |
| Agreement | For R2D sequence-based SIP,<br>- Device obtains sequence length and M value from periodic broadcast information, the followings are to be further selected<br>  - Option 1: Periodic broadcast information indicates one sequence length and one M value per RSRP range<br>    - Opt.1-1: sequence lengths and M values for all configured RSRP ranges are indicated in a given modification period<br>      - When device receiving, device at least use the indicated one sequence length and one M value corresponding to its measured RSRP range to receive, while device is not required to use the indicated one sequence length and one M value corresponding to a higher RSRP range than its measured RSRP to receive<br>        - FFS: whether device can use the indicated one sequence length and one M value corresponding to a lower RSRP range than its measured RSRP to receive<br>    - Opt.1-2: one sequence length and one M value for a given RSRP range from all configured RSRP ranges are indicated in a given modification period<br>      - When device receiving, if device’s measured RSRP is in the given RSRP range, device uses the indicated one sequence length and one M value to receive, while if device’s measured RSRP is lower than the given RSRP range, device is not required to use the indicated one sequence length and one M value to receive<br>        - FFS: if device’s measured RSRP is higher than the given RSRP range, whether device can use the indicated one sequence length and one M value to receive<br>  - Option 2: Periodic broadcast information indicates one sequence length and one M value in a given modification period<br>      - When device receiving, all devices use the indicated one sequence length and one M value to receive | [Chair notes RAN1#126bis_eom1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23126bis_eom1.docx)<br>[R1-2607513](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Docs/R1-2607513.zip) |
| Agreement | For L1 R2D control information,<br>- Device obtains M value, FEC and repetition from periodic broadcast information, the followings are to be further selected<br>  - Option 1: Periodic broadcast information provides one value set of {M value, FEC, repetition} per RSRP range<br>    - Opt.1-1: value sets for {M value, FEC, repetition} for all configured RSRP ranges are provided in a given modification period<br>    - Opt.1-2: one value set of {M value, FEC, repetition} for a given RSRP range from all configured RSRP ranges are provided in a given modification period<br>  - Option 2: Periodic broadcast information provides one value set of {M value, FEC, repetition} in a given modification period<br>  - FFS: whether M value is indicated separately or commonly indicated for sequence-based SIP and L1 R2D control information | [Chair notes RAN1#126bis_eom1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23126bis_eom1.docx)<br>[R1-2607513](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Docs/R1-2607513.zip) |
| Agreement | The time location of the remaining part of broadcast information (i.e. which first part is followed by the remaining part) is determined based on (part of) broadcast information index, which is indicated in the first part | [Chair notes RAN1#126bis_eom1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23126bis_eom1.docx)<br>[R1-2607513](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Docs/R1-2607513.zip) |

| Agreement | the previous agreement is updated as follows<br>\| Agreement: For R2D unmodulated sinusoid single tone CFO calibration signal, the waveform generation is selected from the followings Option 1: DFT-s-OFDM waveform generation is used to generate unmodulated sinusoid single tone CFO calibration signal FFS: necessary specification change for DFT-s-OFDM waveform generation from Rel-19 includes at least A single subcarrier is used in the existing BB generation exponential in TS 38.291 A phase rotation/compensation per OFDM symbol is applied after CP insertion to retain phase continuity cross OFDM symbol boundary Option 2: Waveform generation is up to implementation which needs to retain subcarrier orthogonality of OFDM symbols FFS: how to retain subcarrier orthogonality of OFDM symbols is retained by the followings Define the R2D unmodulated sinusoid single tone CFO calibration signal as where β is a constant value which is non-related to the modulation symbol and the exact value of β can be up to reader implementation, phase continuity is retained over the time duration of ‘t’ The transmission of R2D unmodulated sinusoid single tone CFO calibration start and end both at OFDM symbol boundary \| | [Chair notes RAN1#126bis_eom1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23126bis_eom1.docx)<br>[R1-2607513](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Docs/R1-2607513.zip) |

| Agreement | From device perspective, for the reception of non-periodic R2D transmission, for potential collision handling with periodic R2D transmission,<br>- Device assumes sequence-based SIP [and L1 R2D control information] of non-periodic R2D transmission does not collide with periodic R2D transmission<br>- Device assumes non-periodic R2D transmission is postponed if any part other than sequence-based SIP [and L1 R2D control information] of the non-periodic R2D transmission collides with periodic R2D transmission<br>  - Note: Device may also receive the periodic transmission<br>  - Only the collision part and the followings of non-periodic R2D transmission is postponed | [Chair notes RAN1#126bis_eom1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23126bis_eom1.docx)<br>[R1-2607515](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Docs/R1-2607515.zip) |
| Agreement | From device perspective, for the non-periodic D2R transmission, RAN1 select from the following options for potential collision handling with periodic R2D transmission<br>- Option 1: Device assumes non-periodic D2R transmission does not collide with the reception of periodic R2D transmission<br>- Option 2: Device assumes non-periodic D2R transmission is postponed if any part of the non-periodic D2R transmission collides with the reception of periodic R2D transmission<br>  - Note: Device may or may not receive the periodic R2D transmission and it is up to implementation<br>  - Only the collision part and the followings of non-periodic D2R transmission is postponed<br>    - FFS details including determination of the collision part<br>  - Note: Device assumes D2R preamble of non-periodic D2R transmission does not collide with periodic R2D transmission<br>- Option 3: Device assumes to transmit non-periodic D2R transmission if it collides with the reception of periodic R2D transmission, and device skips the reception of at least the collision part of the periodic R2D transmission<br>  - FFS details on determination of the collision part<br>- Option 4: If non-periodic D2R transmission collides with the reception of periodic R2D transmission, it is up to device implementation for reception or transmission | [Chair notes RAN1#126bis_eom1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23126bis_eom1.docx)<br>[R1-2607515](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Docs/R1-2607515.zip) |
| Agreement | For L1 R2D control information preceding scheduled data payload, support content of L1 R2D control information is carried in CRC mask. RAN1 select which content to be carried in CRC mask from the followings<br>- Option 1: Device ID for device monitoring unicast transmission, or pre-defined ID for device monitoring non-unicast transmission e.g. paging (using CRC length 16)<br>- Option 2: (part of) Physical layer Reader ID<br>  - Option 2-1: 9 bits part of physical layer Reader ID are carried in CRC mask (using CRC length 16)<br>  - Option 2-2: 6 bits part of physical layer Reader ID are carried in CRC mask where the rest 3 bits are in the content as payload (using CRC length 6) | [Chair notes RAN1#126bis_eom1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23126bis_eom1.docx)<br>[R1-2607515](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Docs/R1-2607515.zip) |

9 Release 20 NR › 9.3 Solutions for Ambient IoT (Internet of Things) in NR Phase 2

| 구분 | 내용 | 출처 |
|---|---|---|
| Agreement | For PDRCH scrambling sequence initialization, define , where  denotes the AS-ID and  denotes the physical layer reader ID in the range of 0 to 1007.  is set to 0 for Msg1 in CBRA and first D2R transmission for DO-A to start DO-A traffic transmission. | [Chair notes RAN1#126bis_eom1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23126bis_eom1.docx)<br>[R1-2607546](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Docs/R1-2607546.zip) |

| Agreement | For D2R midamble structure Option 2 for BPSK, support Alt 1, i.e., use the D2R preamble sequence of the same D2R transmission as base sequence of D2R midamble sequence. | [Chair notes RAN1#126bis_eom1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23126bis_eom1.docx)<br>[R1-2607546](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Docs/R1-2607546.zip) |
| Agreement | For D2R midamble structure Option 1 for BPSK, m-sequence is supported as the sequence type (i.e., Alt 1). | [Chair notes RAN1#126bis_eom1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23126bis_eom1.docx)<br>[R1-2607546](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Docs/R1-2607546.zip) |
| Agreement | For Rel-20 D2R transmission, support Option 4 with the following bit duration sets:<br>- For Device 2b: {266.67, 133.33, 66.67, 33.33, 16.67, 8.33, 4.17, 1.39} μs.<br>- For Device C: {266.67, 133.33, 66.67, 33.33, 16.67, 8.33, 4.17, 2.78} μs. | [Chair notes RAN1#126bis_eom1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23126bis_eom1.docx)<br>[R1-2607546](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Docs/R1-2607546.zip) |
| Agreement | For the Option 1 and Option 2 midamble structures for BPSK, down select from following alternatives:<br>- Alt 1:<br>  - Step 1: Determine the Option 2 insertion positions based only on data bits and insert the Option 2 midamble bits.<br>  - Step 2: Determine the Option 1 insertion positions based only on data bits, using the Option 1 insertion interval. If an Option 1 insertion position coincides with an Option 2 insertion position, apply one of the following:<br>    - Replace the Option 2 midamble bit at that position with the Option 1 midamble.<br>- Alt 2:<br>  - Step 1: Determine the Option 2 insertion positions based only on data bits and insert the Option 2 midamble bits.<br>  - Step 2: Determine the Option 1 insertion positions by counting both data bits and the inserted Option 2 midamble bits, using the Option 1 insertion interval, and insert the Option 1 midambles at these positions. | [Chair notes RAN1#126bis_eom1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23126bis_eom1.docx)<br>[R1-2607546](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Docs/R1-2607546.zip) |
| Agreement | For the D2R midamble at the end of PDRCH with BPSK, the following is supported:<br>- Indication of structure of D2R midamble at the end of PDRCH<br>- The structure of D2R midamble at the end of PDRCH is predefined.<br>- Structure of D2R midamble at the end of PDRCH<br>- When a D2R midamble at the end of PDRCH is present,<br>  - The Option 1 midamble is appended at the end of the PDRCH.<br>- Applicability of presence indication for D2R midamble at the end of PDRCH<br>- The reader indicates presence of D2R midamble at the end of PDRCH using a 1-bit field only when Option 1 is enabled. | [Chair notes RAN1#126bis_eom1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23126bis_eom1.docx)<br>[R1-2607546](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Docs/R1-2607546.zip) |
| Agreement | For D2R FDMA where the device selects a frequency resource from an available resource set, define a frequency resource index i = 0, 1, ..., NFDMA−1 in ascending order of frequency, where NFDMA is the number of FDM(A) resources. The corresponding offset index k is determined as follows:<br>- If Alt 1, where k takes positive odd values, is selected:<br>  - k = 2i + 1<br>- If Alt 2, where k can take zero, positive and negative values, is selected:<br>  - k = 2i − (NFDMA − 1).<br>FFS: supported value range of NFDMA.<br>FFS: Whether this mapping also applies to scheduled D2R transmissions.<br>FFS: Information to be provided by the reader for frequency resource configuration and the corresponding indication methods. | [Chair notes RAN1#126bis_eom1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23126bis_eom1.docx)<br>[R1-2607547](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Docs/R1-2607547.zip) |
| Agreement | For D2R FDM(A) Option 1, support Alt 2 for the offset indexing scheme, where k can be zero, positive or negative, and funit is half of the occupied bandwidth.<br>- k is odd (i.e., k = ..., -5, -3, -1, 1, 3, 5, ...) when NFDMA is even.<br>- k is even (i.e., k = ..., -4, -2, 0, 2, 4, ...) when NFDMA is odd.<br>- NFDMA is the number of FDM(A) resources. | [Chair notes RAN1#126bis_eom1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23126bis_eom1.docx)<br>[R1-2607547](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Docs/R1-2607547.zip) |

| Agreement | For D2R midamble structure Option 1 with OOK, support m-sequence as the sequence type. | [Chair notes RAN1#126bis_eom1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23126bis_eom1.docx)<br>[R1-2607547](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Docs/R1-2607547.zip) |
| Agreement | For the D2R midamble with OOK, support sequence lengths of 7 and 31 bits. | [Chair notes RAN1#126bis_eom1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23126bis_eom1.docx)<br>[R1-2607547](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Docs/R1-2607547.zip) |
| Agreement | For Rel-20 D2R preamble, the reader indicates the preamble sequence length using a 2-bit field.<br>- For the first D2R transmission to start DO-A traffic, the field is included in the remaining part of periodic broadcast information.<br>- For other D2R transmissions, the field is included in the D2R scheduling information carried by the corresponding R2D transmission. | [Chair notes RAN1#126bis_eom1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23126bis_eom1.docx)<br>[R1-2607547](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Docs/R1-2607547.zip) |
| Agreement | For Rel-20 D2R transmissions, the midamble configuration information to be indicated by the reader is carried as follows:<br>- For the first D2R transmission to start DO-A traffic, it is included in the remaining part of periodic broadcast information.<br>- For other D2R transmissions, it is included in the D2R scheduling information carried by the corresponding R2D transmission. | [Chair notes RAN1#126bis_eom1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23126bis_eom1.docx)<br>[R1-2607547](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Docs/R1-2607547.zip) |
| Agreement | For D2R midamble insertion structure Option 2 for BPSK, support the following insertion intervals: {S/8, S/4, S/2, S, S×2, S×4, S×6, S×8} bits, where S = 266.67/Tb and Tb is the D2R bit duration in us.<br>  - An insertion interval is applicable only if it corresponds to an integer number of bits. | [Chair notes RAN1#126bis_eom1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23126bis_eom1.docx)<br>[R1-2607547](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Docs/R1-2607547.zip) |
| Agreement | For the D2R midamble Option 1 structure for BPSK, when enabled, the reader indicates N using a 2-bit field, where IOpt1 = N × IOpt2, and IOpt1 and IOpt2 are the insertion intervals of the Option 1 and Option 2 structures, respectively.<br>  - The same set of candidate N values applies to all supported Option 2 insertion intervals. | [Chair notes RAN1#126bis_eom1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23126bis_eom1.docx)<br>[R1-2607547](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Docs/R1-2607547.zip) |
| Agreement | For D2R midamble structure Option 1 with OOK, the following insertion intervals are supported: {48S, 96S, 168S, 240S} bits, where S = 266.67/Tb and Tb is the D2R bit duration in us. | [Chair notes RAN1#126bis_eom1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23126bis_eom1.docx)<br>[R1-2607547](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Docs/R1-2607547.zip) |
| Agreement | For indication of the available frequency resource(s) for D2R FDMA:<br>- For the first D2R transmission to start DO-A traffic, the information is included in the remaining part of periodic broadcast information.<br>- For other D2R transmissions, the information is included in the D2R scheduling information carried by the corresponding R2D transmission. | [Chair notes RAN1#126bis_eom1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23126bis_eom1.docx)<br>[R1-2607547](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Docs/R1-2607547.zip) |
| Agreement | Jointly encode FEC on/off and the block-level repetition number for PDRCH, where repetition larger than 1 is supported only when FEC is enabled. Following entries are supported for the joint encoding.<br>- FEC off, repetition = 1<br>- FEC on, repetition = 1<br>- FEC on, repetition = 2<br>- FEC on, repetition = 4<br>- FEC on, repetition = 8<br>- FEC on, repetition = 16 | [Chair notes RAN1#126bis_eom1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23126bis_eom1.docx)<br>[R1-2607548](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Docs/R1-2607548.zip) |
| Agreement | The information indicated by the reader for determining fref for D2R transmission is carried in the remaining part of periodic broadcast information. | [Chair notes RAN1#126bis_eom1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23126bis_eom1.docx)<br>[R1-2607548](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Docs/R1-2607548.zip) |
| Agreement | For the D2R midamble Option 1 structure for BPSK, down-select one of the following sets of N values:<br>- Alt 1: {100, 200, 400, 800}<br>- Alt 2: {10, 50, 200, 800}<br>- Alt 3: {24, 48, 192, 768} | [Chair notes RAN1#126bis_eom1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23126bis_eom1.docx)<br>[R1-2607548](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Docs/R1-2607548.zip) |
| Agreement | For determination of the D2R reference frequency, consider the following details for down-selection:<br>- For Alt 1,<br>  - the reference frequency indicated by the reader is based on D2R channel raster. With a 10 kHz raster:<br>    - Alt 1-1: Y=19 bits for indexing frequency points over the frequency range below 3 GHz.<br>    - Alt 1-2: Y=13 bits for indexing frequency points within the applicable D2R operating band among n5, n8 and n28.<br>      - FFS: How the device identifies the applicable operating band.<br>      - FFS: How to ensure forward compatibility if additional operating bands are introduced by RAN4.<br>- For Alt 2,<br>  - fR2D_sync_raster ​is determined by the device from the received R2D synchronization signal.<br>    - FFS: How the device identifies the applicable operating band.<br>  - fref = fR2D_sync_raster + foffset,2<br>  - The reader indicates foffset,2,<br>    - foffset,2 has a range of [A, B] MHz<br>      - A = -70, B = -20 for n5<br>      - A = -80, B = -10 for n8<br>      - A = -100, B = -10 for n28<br>      - FFS: How to ensure forward compatibility if additional operating bands are introduced by RAN4.<br>    - foffset,2 requires C bits for indication to the device<br>      - C = 15 bits assuming 5 kHz granularity, 14 bis assuming 10kHz granularity<br>        - FFS: How to support other channel raster granularities, including the corresponding indication bit widths.<br>  - The above offset ranges and TX–RX separation values apply when fR2D_sync_raster is in the DL operating band and fref is in the corresponding UL operating band.<br>    - FFS: Corresponding definitions for R2D transmission in the UL operating band. | [Chair notes RAN1#126bis_eom1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23126bis_eom1.docx)<br>[R1-2607549](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Docs/R1-2607549.zip) |
| Agreement | For D2R FDMA, down-select one or more combinations of alternatives for the following two aspects:<br>- Determination of NFDMA<br>- Alt 1: NFDMA is implicitly derived from the applicable transmission parameters, such as the bit duration and channel bandwidth.<br>  - FFS: The parameters and derivation rule.<br>  - Note: NFDMA is the maximum number of frequency resources<br>- Alt 2: NFDMA is configured and indicated by the reader.<br>  - FFS: The supported values of NFDMA.<br>  - FFS: details<br>  - Note: NFDMA is equal to or smaller than the maximum number of frequency resources<br>- Available frequency resources<br>- Alt A: All resource indices i = 0, 1, ..., NFDMA − 1 are available for selection.<br>- Alt B: The reader indicates a contiguous subset of resource indices among i = 0, 1, ..., NFDMA − 1 that are available for selection.<br>- Alt C: The reader indicates a subset of resource indices among i = 0, 1, ..., NFDMA − 1 that are available for selection. The subset may be contiguous or non-contiguous.<br>Note 1: The following combinations are considered for down-selection:<br>- Alt 1-B, Alt 1-C, Alt 2-A, Alt 2-B, and Alt 2-C.<br>- The combination of Alt 1-A is excluded from consideration.<br>Note 2: The detailed signaling design is left to RAN2. | [Chair notes RAN1#126bis_eom1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23126bis_eom1.docx)<br>[R1-2607549](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Docs/R1-2607549.zip) |
| Agreement | For the D2R preamble at least for device C, the following polynomial and initial state are supported.<br>\| length \| No. \| Polynomial \| Initial state {x₀, x₁, …} \|<br>\| 7 \| 1 \| x³ + x + 1 \| 1 1 1 \|<br>\| 7 \| 2 \| x³ + x² + 1 \| 0 1 0 \|<br>\| 7 \| 3 \| x³ + x + 1 \| 0 1 0 \|<br>\| 31 \| 1 \| x⁵ + x² + 1 \| 0 1 0 0 0 \|<br>\| 31 \| 2 \| x⁵ + x³ + x² + x + 1 \| 1 1 0 0 0 \|<br>\| 31 \| 3 \| x⁵ + x⁴ + x³ + x² + 1 \| 0 1 0 0 0 \|<br>\| 63 \| 1 \| x⁶ + x⁵ + x⁴ + x + 1 \| 0 1 0 0 1 0 \|<br>\| 63 \| 2 \| x⁶ + x + 1 \| 1 1 1 1 0 1 \|<br>\| 63 \| 3 \| x⁶ + x⁵ + 1 \| 0 0 1 0 1 1 \|<br>\| 127 \| 1 \| x⁷ + x⁶ + x⁵ + x⁴ + 1 \| 0 1 0 0 0 1 0 \|<br>\| 127 \| 2 \| x⁷ + x⁴ + 1 \| 1 1 0 0 0 0 1 \|<br>\| 127 \| 3 \| x⁷ + x⁶ + x⁵ + x⁴ + x² + x + 1 \| 0 1 0 0 0 0 1 \|<br>\| 255 \| 1 \| x⁸ + x⁴ + x³ + x² + 1 \| 1 1 0 0 0 0 0 0 \|<br>\| 255 \| 2 \| x⁸ + x⁶ + x³ + x² + 1 \| 1 0 0 0 0 0 0 0 \|<br>\| 255 \| 3 \| x⁸ + x⁶ + x⁴ + x³ + x² + x + 1 \| 0 0 0 1 0 0 0 0 \|<br>- For device 2b<br>  - RAN1 strives to avoid using the same polynomials for R2D PSS/SIP and D2R preambles.<br>  - For each length of D2R preamble which have any shared polynomial with that of R2D SS/SIP, a new set of three D2R sequences can be considered.<br>  - Companies can provide additional sets of D2R preamble sequences for each length before and during the RAN1#127 | [Chair notes RAN1#126bis_eom1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23126bis_eom1.docx)<br>[R1-2607549](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Docs/R1-2607549.zip) |

| Agreement | For device localization RSRP-like measurement, support Option 1 and update the following agreement in RAN1#125:<br>\| Agreement: For device localization RSRP-like measurement, support one of the following options Option 1: Reader indicates a power value from set of values to device, and device uses the corresponding power for D2R transmission. FFSThe set of values is {PCMAX, PCMAX - 3dB, PCMAX - 6dB}. Option 2: Device reports resulting D2R transmission power based on open loop power control FFS details e.g., the D2R transmission power in a quantized value. Y=M-X bits MSB are included in the remaining part of broadcast information.<br>  - X+Y = M = log2(maxN) + log2(maxP)<br>  - Where maxN is the maximum integer multiplier (i.e., 16) used for periodicity of the remaining part, maxP is the maximum integer multiplier used for the periodicity of the modification period.<br>- Alt. 2a: hierarchical/local broadcast information index<br>  - X+Y=M=ceil{log2(N)}+log2(maxP)<br>  - Where,<br>    - Periodicity of remaining BI=N*640ms, N=4, [8, 12],16<br>    - maxP is the maximum integer multiplier used for the periodicity of the modification period | [Chair notes RAN1#126bis_eom1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23126bis_eom1.docx)<br>[R1-2607195](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126b/Docs/R1-2607195.zip) |
